{"forms":[{"form":"Sagnac effects","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A phenomenon encountered in interferometry that is elicited by rotation. The Sagnac effect manifests itself in a setup called a ring interferometer. A beam of light is split and the two beams are made to follow the same path but in opposite directions. To act as a ring the trajectory must enclose an area. On return to the point of entry the two light beams are allowed to exit the ring and undergo interference. The relative phases of the two exiting beams, and thus the position of the interference fringes, are shifted according to the angular velocity of the apparatus."]}],"upstream_line":4907676,"upstream_offset":11523969408,"upstream_record_sha256":"e809f7e380cd0e679438888e752a0232fd288f734e20ad52f6773b94e0b0e7cb","word":"Sagnac effect"}

{"forms":[{"form":"cones","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A surface of revolution formed by rotating a segment of a line around another line that intersects the first line."],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A solid of revolution formed by rotating a triangle around one of its altitudes."],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A space formed by taking the direct product of a given space with a closed interval and identifying all of one end to a point."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences","topology"]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","The fruit of a conifer or a similarly shaped fruit or flower head of certain other plants, such as banksias and proteas."],"synonyms":[{"word":"strobilus"},{"word":"strobile"}]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","A unit of volume, applied solely to marijuana and only while it is in a smokable state; roughly 1.5 cubic centimetres, depending on use."]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","An ice cream cone."]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","A traffic cone."]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","A traffic cone.","A passenger on a cruise ship (that needs to be navigated around)."],"tags":["broadly","slang"]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","A cone-shaped cannabis joint."],"tags":["slang"]},{"glosses":["Anything in the general shape of a cone.","A shell of the genus Conus, having a conical form."]},{"glosses":["Any of the small cone-shaped structures in the retina."],"topics":["anatomy","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["The bowl piece on a bong."],"tags":["slang"]},{"glosses":["The bowl piece on a bong.","The process of smoking cannabis in a bong."],"tags":["slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A cone is an object (the apex) and a natural transformation from a constant functor (whose image is the apex of the cone and its identity morphism) to a diagram functor. Its components are projections from the apex to the objects of the diagram and it has a “naturality triangle” for each morphism in the diagram. (A “naturality triangle” is just a naturality square which is degenerate at its apex side.)","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An object V together with an arrow going from V to each object of a diagram such that for any arrow A in the diagram, the pair of arrows from V which subtend A also commute with it. (Then V can be said to be the cone’s vertex and the diagram which the cone subtends can be said to be its base.)"],"topics":["category-theory","computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A set of formal languages with certain desirable closure properties, in particular those of the regular languages, the context-free languages and the recursively enumerable languages."],"topics":["computing","computing-theory","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈkəʊn/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkoʊn/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkon/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkoːn/","tags":["Wales"]}],"synonyms":[{"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"],"word":"conical surface"},{"sense":"ice cream cone","word":"cornet"},{"sense":"ice cream cone","word":"ice cream cone"}],"upstream_line":259632,"upstream_offset":1292833616,"upstream_record_sha256":"65485d52617dadab132a5a456c2e37af0693484e64bca2a0656c3b218319557f","word":"cone"}
